Third-party licenses for the bundled Monocle compatibility build

The adapted Monocle source has its own accompanying MIT license. Dependencies
retain the licenses identified below. This inventory conservatively includes
all 142 non-development dependency packages reachable from the locked WASM
build, including build-time packages; it does not assert that every package
contributes code to the optimized artifact. Full license/notice texts are
retained below, deduplicating only byte-identical files by their SHA-256.
Registry source identities and package checksums are fixed in Cargo.lock.

MPL 2.0 source availability

Unmodified complete source packages accompany this repository in mpl-sources/:
colored 3.1.1:
https://static.crates.io/crates/colored/colored-3.1.1.crate
SHA-256 faf9468729b8cbcea668e36183cb69d317348c2e08e994829fb56ebfdfbaac34
option-ext 0.2.0:
https://static.crates.io/crates/option-ext/option-ext-0.2.0.crate
SHA-256 04744f49eae99ab78e0d5c0b603ab218f515ea8cfe5a456d7629ad883a3b6e7d
Both source packages and their full MPL 2.0 licenses are available at these
versioned public URLs. The bundled archives match Cargo.lock and SHA256SUMS.
These dependencies are unmodified. Preserve this source availability notice
and the applicable licenses when redistributing the compiled plugin.

<p>This file describes the copyright and licensing information for the source code within The Rust Project git tree related to the Rust Standard Library, and the third-party dependencies used when building the Rust Standard Library.</p>
